\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq: q-phase condition}}{\leq}c\left(\frac{2}{\delta}\right)^{\theta}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)^{\theta}(|Du|+|F|)^{\theta q}\,dz.

Finally, using Hölder’s inequality and combining the above estimates, we obtain the desired estimate in the statement. This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 3.9
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Then there exists a constant
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})\geq 1
such that for any
J_{4\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}
with (
3.6
),
\tau\in[2\rho,4\rho]
and
\theta\in((q-1)/p,1]
, we have

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\frac{|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};\tau,\lambda}|^{\theta p}}{\tau^{\theta p}}\;dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}(H(z,|Du|))^{\theta}\;dz+c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\;dz\right)^{\theta}.

Proof.

From
2.8
of
Lemma

2.6
and
Lemma

3.7
, we find

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\frac{|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};\tau,\lambda}|^{\theta p}}{\tau^{\theta p}}\;dz

\displaystyle\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}|Du|^{\theta p}\;dz+c\underbrace{\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\left(\lambda^{2-q}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)(|Du|+|F|)^{q-1}\;dz\right)^{\theta p}}_{\mathrm{I}}

\displaystyle\quad+c\underbrace{\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\left(\lambda^{1-q+\frac{q}{p}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}(|Du|+|F|)^{p-1}\;dz\right)^{\theta p}}_{\mathrm{II}}

\displaystyle\quad+c\underbrace{\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\lambda^{(2-q)\theta p}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}(|Du|+|F|)^{p-1}\;dz\right)^{\theta p}}_{\mathrm{III}}.

Estimate of
\mathrm{I}
:
We use the first and second conditions of (
3.6
), (
3.7
) and
b(z)\leq||b||_{L^{\infty}(\Omega_{T})}
to estimate

\displaystyle\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\left(\lambda^{2-q}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)(|Du|+|F|)^{q-1}\;dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq: q-phase condition}}{\leq}K^{-\theta}b(z_{0})^{\theta}\lambda^{(q-p)\theta}\lambda^{(2-q)\theta p}||b||_{L^{\infty}}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz\right)^{\frac{(q-1)p}{q}}

\displaystyle=c(K,||b||_{L^{\infty}},\theta)\lambda^{(q-p+2p-pq)\theta}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz\right)^{\frac{(q-1)p-q}{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{EQQ3.5}}{\leq}c(K,||b||_{L^{\infty}},\theta)\lambda^{(q-p+2p-pq+(q-1)p-q)\theta}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz

\displaystyle=c(K,||b||_{L^{\infty}},\theta){{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq: q-phase condition}}{\leq}\frac{c(K,||b||_{L^{\infty}},\theta)}{b(z_{0})^{\theta}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)^{\theta}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c(K,||b||_{L^{\infty}},\theta,\delta){{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}(H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|))^{\theta}\,dz.

Here we note that, since
2\leq p\leq q,

\displaystyle\frac{p+q}{pq}=\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{q}\leq 1\implies p+q\leq pq\implies q\leq p(q-1).

Estimate of
\mathrm{II}
:
We use the first and third conditions of (
3.6
) and Hölder’s inequality to deduce

\displaystyle\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\left(\lambda^{1-q+\frac{q}{p}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}(|Du|+|F|)^{p-1}\;dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\leq K^{-\theta}b(z_{0})^{\theta}\lambda^{(q-p)\theta}\lambda^{\left(1-q+\frac{q}{p}\right)\theta p}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\theta}(|Du|+|F|)^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{p-1}

\displaystyle=c\lambda^{(q-p)\theta}\lambda^{\left(1-q+\frac{q}{p}\right)\theta p}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\theta}(|Du|+|F|)^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{p-2}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\theta}(|Du|+|F|)^{\theta p}\,dz

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq: q-phase condition}}{\leq}c(K,\theta,||b||_{L^{\infty}})\lambda^{(q-p+p-pq+q+pq-2q)\theta}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\theta}(|Du|+|F|)^{\theta p}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c(K,\theta,||b||_{L^{\infty}}){{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}}}(H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|))^{\theta}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left[{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz+\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz\right)^{\theta}\right].

Estimate of
\mathrm{III}
:
Similarly, using Hölder’s inequality and (
3.7
), we can estimate

\displaystyle\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\lambda^{(2-q)\theta p}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\omega_{a}(\tau)(|Du|+|F|)^{p-1}\;dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{(2-q)\theta p}\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}(|Du|+|F|)^{\theta p}\;dz\right)^{p-1}

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{(2-q)\theta p}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}(|Du|+|F|)^{q}\;dz\right)^{\frac{(p-2)\theta p}{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}(|Du|+|F|)^{\theta p}\;dz

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{EQQ3.5}}{\leq}c\lambda^{(2-q)\theta p}\lambda^{(p-2)\theta p}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\theta}(|Du|+|F|)^{\theta p}\;dz

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{(p-q)\theta p}\left[{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz+\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz\right)^{\theta}\right].

Combining the three estimates above, we conclude the proof of this lemma.
∎

Lemma 3.10
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Then for
J_{c_{v}(2\rho),\lambda}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}
with (
3.6
), there exists a constant
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})\geq 1
such that

S(u)^{q}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}=\sup_{I^{q}_{2\rho,\lambda}(t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}\frac{\left|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{2}}{(2\rho)^{2}}\;dx\leq c\lambda^{2}.

Proof.

Let
2\rho\leq\rho_{1}<\rho_{2}\leq 4\rho
. By
Lemma

2.4
, there exists a constant
c
depending on
n,p,q,\nu
and
L
such that

\displaystyle\lambda^{q-2}S(u)^{q}_{z_{0};\rho_{1},\lambda}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c\rho_{2}^{q}}{(\rho_{2}-\rho_{1})^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(a(z)\frac{|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{p}}{\rho^{p}_{2}}+b(z)\frac{|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{q}}{\rho^{q}_{2}}\right)dz

(3.8)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{c\rho_{2}^{2}\lambda^{q-2}}{(\rho_{2}-\rho_{1})^{2}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{2}}{\rho_{2}^{2}}\;dz+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\;dz.

Firstly, using
Lemma

3.8
with
\theta=1
and
b(z)\leq||b||_{L^{\infty}}
, we get

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{q}}{\rho^{q}_{2}}\,dz\leq cb(z_{0}){{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}[H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)]\,dz\overset{\eqref{eq: q-phase condition}}{\leq}c||b||^{2}_{L^{\infty}(\Omega_{T})}\lambda^{q}.

Next, we estimate

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{p}}{\rho^{p}_{2}}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{p}}{\rho^{p}_{2}}\,dz

\displaystyle\quad+\omega_{a}(\tau){{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{p}}{\rho^{p}_{2}}\,dz.

Now the first term to the above inequality can be estimated by
Lemma

3.9
with
\theta=1.
Indeed, using the third condition of (
3.6
), we get

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{p}}{\rho^{p}_{2}}\,dz\leq b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}.

Now we estimate the second term. Again, from
2.8
and
Lemma

3.7
, we have

(3.9)

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\omega_{a}(\tau)\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0};\tau,\lambda}|^{p}}{\tau^{p}}\;dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq c\underbrace{{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\omega_{a}(\tau)|Du|^{p}\;dz}_{\mathrm{I}}+c\omega_{a}(\tau)\underbrace{\left(\lambda^{2-q}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)(|Du|+|F|)^{q-1}\;dz\right)^{p}}_{\mathrm{II}}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+c\omega_{a}(\tau)\underbrace{\left(\lambda^{1-q+\frac{q}{p}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}(|Du|+|F|)^{p-1}\;dz\right)^{p}}_{\mathrm{III}}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+c\omega_{a}(\tau)\underbrace{\lambda^{(2-q)p}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}(|Du|+|F|)^{p-1}\;dz\right)^{p}}_{\mathrm{IV}}.

Estimate of
\mathrm{I}
:
We simply use Hölder’s inequality and (
3.7
):

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\omega_{a}(\tau)|Du|^{p}\,dz\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}|Du|^{q}\,dz\right)^{\frac{p}{q}}\overset{\eqref{EQQ3.5}}{\leq}c\lambda^{p}\leq c\lambda^{q}.

Estimate of
\mathrm{II}
:
We use Hölder’s inequality, (
3.7
) and
b(z)\leq||b||_{L^{\infty}}
to deduce

\displaystyle\left(\lambda^{2-q}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)(|Du|+|F|)^{q-1}\;dz\right)^{p}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\lambda^{(2-q)p}||b||_{L^{\infty}}^{p}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q}\,dz\right)^{\frac{(q-1)p}{q}}\overset{\eqref{EQQ3.5}}{\leq}c\lambda^{p}\leq c\lambda^{q}.

Estimate of
\mathrm{III}
:
Using Hölder’s inequality and third condition of (
3.6
), we get

\displaystyle\left(\lambda^{1-q+\frac{q}{p}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}(|Du|+|F|)^{p-1}\;dz\right)^{p}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{\left(1-q+\frac{q}{p}\right)p}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)(|Du|+|F|)^{p}\,dz\right)^{p-1}\leq c\lambda^{\left(1-q+\frac{q}{p}\right)p}\lambda^{q(p-1)}=c\lambda^{p}\leq c\lambda^{q}.

Estimate of
\mathrm{IV}
:
Again, we simply use Hölder’s inequality and (
3.7
) to obtain

\displaystyle\lambda^{(2-q)p}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}(|Du|+|F|)^{p-1}\;dz\right)^{p}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda^{(2-q)p}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}(|Du|+|F|)^{q}\;dz\right)^{\frac{(p-1)p}{q}}\overset{\eqref{EQQ3.5}}{\leq}c\lambda^{2p-pq+p^{2}-p}\leq c\lambda^{q}.

In the last inequality above, we used

\displaystyle 0\geq(p+1)(p-q)=p^{2}-pq+p-q=p^{2}-pq+2p-p-q\implies 2p-pq+p^{2}-p\leq q.

Now we estimate the second term of (
3.8
). Using
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=2,s=q,r=2,\vartheta=\frac{1}{2},

Lemma

3.8
and
Eq.

3.6
, we estimate the following

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{2}}{\rho_{2}^{2}}\;dz

\displaystyle\leq c\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{I_{\rho_{2}}(t_{0})}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{\rho_{2}}(x_{0})}\left(\frac{|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{q}}{\rho_{2}^{q}}+|Du|^{q}\right)\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}dt\,\left(S(u)^{q}_{z_{0},\rho_{2},\lambda}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq c(\delta)\lambda\left(S(u)^{q}_{z_{0},\rho_{2},\lambda}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Note that, in the last step of the above estimate we also use the second condition of (
3.6
), specifically,
b(z_{0})\geq\frac{\delta}{2}
and
\frac{b(z)}{3}\leq b(z_{0})\leq 3b(z)
. Since the third condition of (
3.6
) implies that

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz\lesssim\lambda^{q},

combining all the estimates above, we have

\displaystyle S(u)^{q}_{z_{0},\rho_{1},\lambda}\leq c\frac{\rho^{q}_{2}}{(\rho_{2}-\rho_{1})^{q}}\lambda^{2}+c\frac{\rho^{2}_{2}}{(\rho_{2}-\rho_{1})^{2}}\lambda\left(S(u)^{q}_{z_{0},\rho_{2},\lambda}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Now applying Young’s inequality in the second term on the right-hand side above, we obtain

\displaystyle S(u)^{q}_{z_{0},\rho_{1},\lambda}\leq\frac{1}{2}S(u)^{q}_{z_{0},\rho_{2},\lambda}+c\left[\frac{\rho^{q}_{2}}{(\rho_{2}-\rho_{1})^{q}}+\frac{\rho^{4}_{2}}{(\rho_{2}-\rho_{1})^{4}}\right]\lambda^{2}.

Now we use iteration lemma,
Lemma

2.2
to complete the proof.
∎

Remark 3.1
.

It is interesting to note that the term in (
3.9
) admits a sharper estimate. Indeed, we have

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\omega_{a}(\tau)\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0};\tau,\lambda}|^{p}}{\tau^{p}}\;dz\leq c(\delta)\omega_{a}(\tau)\lambda^{p}.

This shows that an improved bound can be derived. Regarding the estimate of the fourth term (see Estimate of
\mathrm{IV}
above), we observe that the condition
p\leq q
implies
p(1-q+p)\leq p,
which subsequently yields the bound
c(\delta)\lambda^{p}
.

Lemma 3.11
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Then for
J_{c_{v}(2\rho),\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)\subset\Omega_{T}
with (
3.6
), there exist constants
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})\geq 1
and
\theta_{0}=\theta_{0}(n,p,q,s)\in(0,1)
such that for any
\theta\in\left(\theta_{0},1\right)
, we have

\displaystyle{{{\displaystyle\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}H\left(z,\frac{\left|u-(u)_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|}{2\rho}\right)dz

\displaystyle\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}\left(\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\frac{\left|u-(u)_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{\theta p}}{(2\rho)^{\theta p}}+\inf_{J_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}|Du|^{\theta p}\right)dz\;

\displaystyle\times\lambda^{(q-p)(1-\theta)}\left[S(u)_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right]^{\frac{(1-\theta)p}{2}}

\displaystyle+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}\left(\frac{\left|u-(u)_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{\theta q}}{(2\rho)^{\theta q}}+|Du|^{\theta q}\right)dz\;\left(S(u)^{q}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)q}{2}}

\displaystyle+\omega_{a}(2\rho){{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{\left|u-(u)_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{p}}{(2\rho)^{p}}\,dz.

Proof.

Using the second condition of (
3.6
) and uniform continuity of
a(\cdot)
, we have

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}H\left(z,\frac{\left|u-(u)_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|}{2\rho}\right)dz\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}a(z)\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{p}}{(2\rho)^{p}}+b(z_{0})\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{q}}{(2\rho)^{q}}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\underbrace{{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{p}}{(2\rho)^{p}}\,dz}_{\mathrm{I}}+c\underbrace{{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z_{0})\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{q}}{(2\rho)^{q}}\,dz}_{\mathrm{II}}

\displaystyle\quad+c\omega_{a}(2\rho){{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{p}}{(2\rho)^{p}}\,dz.

We estimate the terms
\mathrm{I}
and
\mathrm{II}
below.

Estimate of
\mathrm{I}
:
We use
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=p,s=\theta p,\vartheta=\theta,r=2
to obtain

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{p}}{(2\rho)^{p}}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{\theta p}}{(2\rho)^{\theta p}}+\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}|Du|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{1-\theta}[S(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}]^{\frac{(1-\theta)p}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{\theta p}}{(2\rho)^{\theta p}}+\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}|Du|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\lambda^{(q-p)(1-\theta)}\left(S(u)^{q}_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)p}{2}}.

Estimate of
\mathrm{II}
:
Similarly, using
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=q,s=\theta q,\vartheta=\theta,r=2,
we get

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z_{0})\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{q}}{(2\rho)^{q}}\,dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}\left(\frac{\left|u-(u)_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{\theta q}}{(2\rho)^{\theta q}}+|Du|^{\theta q}\,\right)dz\;\left(S(u)^{q}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)q}{2}}.

This completes the proof.
∎

Now we are ready to prove the reverse Hölder inequality for
q
-phase.

Lemma 3.12
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Then for
J_{c_{v}(2\rho),\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)\subset\Omega_{T}
with (
3.6
), there exist constants
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})\geq 1
and
\theta_{0}=\theta_{0}(n,p,q)\in(0,1)
such that for any
\theta\in(\theta_{0},1),

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|Du|)\;dz\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}[H(z,|Du|)]^{\theta}\;dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\;dz.

Proof.

From the energy estimate,
Lemma

2.4
, we have

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|Du|)\;dz

\displaystyle\leq c\underbrace{{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H\left(z,\frac{|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|}{2\rho}\right)dz}_{\mathrm{I}}

\displaystyle\quad+c\underbrace{\lambda^{q-2}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{2}}{(2\rho)^{2}}\;dz}_{\mathrm{II}}+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\;dz.

Estimate of
\mathrm{I}
:
It follows from
Lemma

3.11
and
Lemma

3.10
that

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H\left(z,\frac{|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|}{2\rho}\right)dz

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{(1-\theta)q}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\tau}(z_{0})}}\left(\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}\frac{\left|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{\theta p}}{(2\rho)^{\theta p}}+\frac{\left|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{\theta q}}{(2\rho)^{\theta q}}\right)dz

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda^{(1-\theta)q}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(\inf_{\tilde{z}\in J_{2\rho,\tau}(z_{0})}a(\tilde{z})^{\theta}|Du|^{\theta p}+|Du|^{\theta q}\right)dz+\omega_{a}(2\rho){{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{\left|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{p}}{(2\rho)^{p}}\,dz.

To estimate the first term of the above inequality, we use
Lemma

3.9
and
Lemma

3.8
. Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H\left(z,\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|}{2\rho}\right)dz

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{(1-\theta)q}\left[c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\;dz+c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\;dz\right)^{\theta}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\omega_{a}(2\rho){{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{\left|u-(u)_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{p}}{(2\rho)^{p}}\,dz.

Moreover, from the estimates of
Lemma

3.10
, we also have

\displaystyle\omega_{a}(2\rho){{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{\left|u-(u)_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{p}}{(2\rho)^{p}}\,dz\leq c\omega_{a}(2\rho)\lambda^{q}\leq\varepsilon_{1}\lambda^{q}\,\,\,\,\text{for small}\,\,\,\rho>0.

Next, an application of Young’s inequality with
\left(\frac{1}{\theta},\frac{1}{1-\theta}\right)
gives

\displaystyle\lambda^{(1-\theta)q}\left[c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\;dz+c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\;dz\right)^{\theta}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\varepsilon_{2}\lambda^{q}+c(\varepsilon_{2})\left[\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}+{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz\right].

Thus, combining the above estimates, we finally get

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H\left(z,\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|}{2\rho}\right)\;dz\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{q}

\displaystyle+c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz,

where
\varepsilon=\max\left\{\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2}\right\}.

Estimate of
\mathrm{II}
:
Choosing
\sigma=2,s=\theta q,r=2,\vartheta=\frac{1}{2},
in
Lemma

2.1
, we get

\displaystyle\lambda^{q-2}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}|^{2}}{(2\rho)^{2}}\;dz

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{q-2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{I_{2\rho}(t_{0})}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}\left(\frac{|u-(u)^{q}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}|^{\theta q}}{(2\rho)^{\theta q}}+|Du|^{\theta q}\right)\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta q}}dt\,\left(S(u)^{q}_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq c\lambda^{q-1}\left[{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz+\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz\right)^{\theta}\right]^{\frac{1}{\theta q}}.

Note that, in the last step, we used
Lemma

3.10
,
Lemma

3.8
and the second condition of (
3.6
). Now applying Young’s inequality with
\left(q,\frac{q}{q-1}\right)
, we get

\displaystyle\lambda^{q-2}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}|^{2}}{(2\rho)^{2}}\;dz\leq\varepsilon\lambda^{q}+c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz.

Hence, combining all the above estimates and absorbing
\varepsilon\lambda^{q}
by the last condition of (
3.6
) in the left-hand side, we complete the proof.
∎

3.3.
Reverse Hölder inequality for
(p,q)
-phase

In this subsection, we prove reverse Hölder inequality for
(p,q)
-phase. In the
(p,q)
-intrinsic case, we consider the following assumptions:

(3.10)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &Ka(z_{0})\lambda^{p}\leq b(z_{0})\lambda^{q},\\
&\frac{a(z)}{3}\leq a(z_{0})\leq 3a(z)\quad\text{and}\quad\frac{b(z)}{3}\leq b(z_{0})\leq 3b(z)\ \,\text{for all}\,\,\,z\in G_{\rho,\lambda}(z_{0}),\\
&{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}[H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)]\;dz<a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\ \,\text{for every }\tau\in(\rho,c_{v}(2\rho)],\\
&{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}[H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)]\;dz=a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q},\end{aligned}\right.

or

(3.11)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &Ka(z_{0})\lambda^{p}\geq b(z_{0})\lambda^{q},\\
&\frac{a(z)}{3}\leq a(z_{0})\leq 3a(z)\quad\text{and}\quad\frac{b(z)}{3}\leq b(z_{0})\leq 3b(z)\ \,\text{for all}\,\,\,z\in G_{\rho,\lambda}(z_{0}),\\
&{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}[H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)]\;dz<a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\ \,\text{for every }\tau\in(\rho,c_{v}(2\rho)],\\
&{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}[H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)]\;dz=a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}.\end{aligned}\right.

Remark 3.2
.

Note that the second assumption of the above conditions (
3.10
)–(
3.11
) is related to the strict positivity of the coefficients, i.e.,

\inf_{z\in G_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}a(z)>0\quad\text{and}\quad\inf_{z\in G_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}b(z)>0

in the
(p,q)
-intrinsic case. However, in contrast to the
p
-intrinsic or the
q
-intrinsic case, we cannot guarantee a strictly positive uniform lower bound for these quantities (see Subsection
4.1.3
for further details). Therefore, in this case, it is tempting not to impose any restriction on the simultaneous smallness of the competing coefficients
a(\cdot)
and
b(\cdot)
. On the other hand, if
0<b(\cdot)<\varepsilon
for some
\varepsilon>0,
from assumption
a(\cdot)+b(\cdot)\geq\delta,
we get
a(\cdot)\geq\delta-\varepsilon.
Heuristically speaking, when
b(\cdot)
is sufficiently small, the system exhibits
p
-growth behavior, and similarly, when
a(\cdot)
is sufficiently small, it exhibits
q
-growth behavior. However, the conditions in (
3.10
)–(
3.11
) cannot be directly reduced to either the
p
-intrinsic or
q
-intrinsic case and require separate treatment.

Lemma 3.13
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Then there exists a constant
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})\geq 1
such that for any
G_{4\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}
with (
3.10
)–(
3.11
),
\tau\in[2\rho,4\rho]
and
\theta\in((q-1)/p,1]
, we have

(3.12)

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\tau,\lambda}|^{\theta p}}{\tau^{\theta p}}\;dz\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz

and

(3.13)

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\tau,\lambda}|^{\theta q}}{\tau^{\theta q}}\;dz\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz.

Proof.

We observe from (
2.8
) of
Lemma

2.6
that

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0},\tau,\lambda}|^{\theta p}}{\tau^{\theta p}}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}|Du|^{\theta p}\,dz

\displaystyle\quad+c\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\left(|Du|^{p-1}+|F|^{p-1}\right)+b(z)\left(|Du|^{q-1}+|F|^{q-1}\right)dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}|Du|^{\theta p}\,dz+c\underbrace{\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p-1}dz\right)^{\theta p}}_{\mathrm{I}}

(3.14)

\displaystyle\quad+c\underbrace{\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q-1}dz\right)^{\theta p}}_{\mathrm{II}}.

Case I:
In this case, we estimate
\mathrm{I}
and
\mathrm{II}
of (
3.14
) considering the assumption (
3.10
). First we observe, since
b(z_{0})\neq 0,
from the first, second and the third conditions of (
3.10
), we get

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z_{0})\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q}\,dz\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\,b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}.

Thus, we have

(3.15)

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q}\,dz\leq c\lambda^{q}.

Estimate of
\mathrm{I}
:
Using the second condition of (
3.10
) and Hölder’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}a(z_{0})^{\theta}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}a(z_{0})^{\theta}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\theta}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{p-1}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{\theta(p-2)}a(z_{0})^{2\theta}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz.

Now using
a(z_{0})\leq\frac{1}{K}b(z_{0})\lambda^{q-p}
from the assumption (
3.10
), we have

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{\theta(p-2)}a(z_{0})^{2\theta}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{p}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{2\theta}b(z_{0})^{2\theta}\lambda^{2\theta(q-p)}=\left(\frac{b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{2\theta}\leq 1.

Thus we finally obtain

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta p}\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz.

Estimate of
\mathrm{II}
:
Similarly, using the second condition of (
3.10
) and Hölder’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}b(z_{0})}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q}\,dz\right)^{\frac{(q-2)\theta p}{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}b(z_{0})}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\lambda^{(q-2)\theta p}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz.

Thus, combining the above estimates, we obtain (
3.12
).

Case II:
In this case, we estimate
\mathrm{I}
and
\mathrm{II}
of (
3.14
) considering the assumption (
3.11
). Since
a(z_{0})\neq 0,
using the first, second and the third assumptions of (
3.11
), we get

\displaystyle a(z_{0}){{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p}\,dz\leq 3{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}

\displaystyle\leq ca(z_{0})\lambda^{p},

and thus we conclude that

(3.16)

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p}\,dz\leq c\lambda^{p}.

The estimates in this case are similar to those in Case I.

Estimate of
\mathrm{I}
:
Using the second condition of (
3.11
), (
3.16
) and Hölder’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\lambda^{\theta p(p-2)}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz

\displaystyle=c\left(\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz.

Estimate of
\mathrm{II}
:
Similarly, we estimate

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta p}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}b(z_{0})}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p}\,dz\right)^{\theta(q-2)}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\lambda^{q}b(z_{0})}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta p}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz\leq{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz.

Thus, combining the above estimates, we obtain (
3.12
).

Next we prove the estimate (
3.13
). Again, it follows from (
2.8
) of
Lemma

2.6
that

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0},\tau,\lambda}|^{\theta q}}{\tau^{\theta q}}\,dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}|Du|^{\theta q}\,dz+c\underbrace{\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta q}}_{\mathrm{I}}

(3.17)

\displaystyle\qquad+c\underbrace{\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta q}}_{\mathrm{II}}.

Case I:
In this case, we estimate
\mathrm{I}
and
\mathrm{II}
of (
3.17
) considering the assumption (
3.10
).

Estimate of
\mathrm{I}
:
Using the second condition of (
3.10
), Hölder’s inequality and (
3.15
), we see that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta q}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}a(z_{0})}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz\right)^{p-1}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}a(z_{0})}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q}\,dz\right)^{\theta(p-2)}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz.

Estimate of
\mathrm{II}
:
Similarly, we can estimate

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta q}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}b(z_{0})}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q}\,dz\right)^{\theta(q-2)}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\lambda^{q}b(z_{0})}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz.

Combining the above estimates completes the proof of (
3.13
).

Case II:
We estimate
\mathrm{I}
and
\mathrm{II}
of (
3.17
) by considering the assumption (
3.11
).

Estimate of
\mathrm{I}
:
Using (
3.16
), Hölder’s inequality and the second condition of (
3.11
), we get

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta q}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{(p-1)q}{p}}

\displaystyle=c\left(\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\,\right)^{\theta q}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{(p-2)q}{p}}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{q}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}\lambda^{\theta q(p-2)}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}dz

\displaystyle=c\left(\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right){{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}dz.

Estimate of
\mathrm{II}
:
Similarly, we estimate

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta q}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}b(z_{0})^{\theta}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)^{\frac{q-1}{q}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta q}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}b(z_{0})^{\theta}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)^{\theta}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}dz\right)^{q-1}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{\theta(q-2)}b(z_{0})^{2\theta}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}dz.

As in the previous case, using the first condition of (
3.11
), i.e.,
Ka(z_{0})\lambda^{p}\geq b(z_{0})\lambda^{q},
we obtain

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}\right)^{\theta q}(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{\theta(q-2)}b(z_{0})^{2\theta}\leq 1.

It follows that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}}{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q-1}\,dz\right)^{\theta q}\leq c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}dz.

Thus, combining the above estimates, we obtain (
3.13
) for this case.
∎

Next, we prove the following:

Lemma 3.14
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Then for
G_{c_{v}(2\rho),\lambda}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}
with (
3.10
)–(
3.11
), there exists a constant
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})\geq 1
such that

\displaystyle S(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}=\sup_{I^{(p,q)}_{2\rho,\lambda}(t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}\frac{\left|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{2}}{(2\rho)^{2}}\;dx\leq c\lambda^{2}.

Proof.

Let
2\rho\leq\rho_{1}<\rho_{2}\leq 4\rho
. By
Lemma

2.4
, there exists a constant
c
depending on
n,p,q,\nu
and
L
such that

\displaystyle\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p-2}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}\right)S(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\rho_{1},\lambda}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c\rho_{2}^{q}}{(\rho_{2}-\rho_{1})^{q}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(a(z)\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{p}}{\rho^{p}_{2}}+b(z)\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{q}}{\rho^{q}_{2}}\right)dz

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{c\rho_{2}^{2}\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p-2}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}\right)}{(\rho_{2}-\rho_{1})^{2}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{2}}{\rho_{2}^{2}}\;dz

(3.18)

\displaystyle\quad+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\;dz.

Using
Lemma

3.13
with
\theta=1
, we estimate the first term of (
3.18
) as follows:

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(a(z)\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{p}}{\rho^{p}_{2}}+b(z)\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{q}}{\rho^{q}_{2}}\right)dz

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{Eq 3.8}\,\,\text{or}\,\,\eqref{Eq 3.9}}{\leq}c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(a(z_{0})\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{p}}{\rho^{p}_{2}}+b(z_{0})\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{q}}{\rho^{q}_{2}}\right)dz

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{Lemma 3.13}}{\leq}c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z_{0})\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p}+b(z_{0})\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q}\,dz

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{Eq 3.8}\,\,\text{or}\,\,\eqref{Eq 3.9}}{\leq}c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{p}+b(z)\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{q}\,dz\overset{\eqref{Eq 3.8}\,\,\text{or}\,\,\eqref{Eq 3.9}}{\leq}c\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\right).

For the case (
3.10
), using
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=2,s=q,r=2,\vartheta=\frac{1}{2},
we get

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{2}}{\rho_{2}^{2}}\;dz\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{q}}{\rho_{2}^{q}}+|Du|^{q}\right)dz\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\left(S(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0},\rho_{2},\lambda}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Now using (
3.13
) of
Lemma

3.13
and (
3.15
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{q}}{\rho_{2}^{q}}+|Du|^{q}\right)dz\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\leq c\lambda.

On the other hand, for the case (
3.11
), similarly, we derive

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{2}}{\rho_{2}^{2}}\;dz\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{p}}{\rho_{2}^{p}}+|Du|^{p}\right)dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\left(S(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0},\rho_{2},\lambda}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Now using (
3.12
) of
Lemma

3.13
and (
3.16
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\left(\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{p}}{\rho_{2}^{p}}+|Du|^{p}\right)dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq c\lambda.

Thus, together with the above estimates, we get

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{2},\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0};\rho_{2},\lambda}|^{2}}{\rho_{2}^{2}}\;dz\leq\lambda\left(S(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Now applying Young’s inequality and iteration lemma,
Lemma

2.2
, we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma 3.15
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Then for
G_{c_{v}(2\rho),\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)\subset\Omega_{T}
with (
3.10
)–(
3.11
), there exist constants
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})\geq 1
and
\theta_{0}=\theta_{0}(n,p,q)\in(0,1)
such that for any
\theta\in\left(\theta_{0},1\right)
, we have

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}H\left(z,\frac{\left|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|}{2\rho}\right)dz

(3.19)

\displaystyle\leq c\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\right)^{1-\theta}\left[{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz+\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz\right)^{\theta}\right].

Proof.

We use
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=p,s=\theta p,\vartheta=\theta,r=2
to deduce

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\frac{\left|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{p}}{(2\rho)^{p}}\,dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z_{0})^{\theta}\frac{\left|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{\theta p}}{(2\rho)^{\theta p}}+a(z_{0})^{\theta}|Du|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)a(z_{0})^{1-\theta}\left(S(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)p}{2}}.

Now we use
Lemma

3.13
and
Lemma

3.14
to get

\displaystyle\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z_{0})^{\theta}\frac{\left|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{\theta p}}{(2\rho)^{\theta p}}+a(z_{0})^{\theta}|Du|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)a(z_{0})^{1-\theta}\left(S(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}\right)^{\frac{(1-\theta)p}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq c(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p})^{1-\theta}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\theta}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz.

Hence, it follows that

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)\frac{\left|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{p}}{(2\rho)^{p}}\,dz\leq c(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p})^{1-\theta}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\theta}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)\frac{\left|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0};2\rho,\lambda}\right|^{q}}{(2\rho)^{q}}\,dz\leq c(b(z_{0})\lambda^{q})^{1-\theta}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}b(z)^{\theta}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta q}\,dz.

Combining the above two estimates and using Hölder’s inequality, we get the desired estimate (
3.19
).
∎

Lemma 3.16
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Then for
G_{c_{v}(2\rho),\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)\subset\Omega_{T}
with (
3.10
)–(
3.11
), there exist constants
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})\geq 1
and
\theta_{0}=\theta_{0}(n,p,q)\in(0,1)
such that for any
\theta\in(\theta_{0},1),

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}[H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)]\;dz

\displaystyle\qquad\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}[H(z,|Du|)]^{\theta}\;dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\;dz.

Proof.

From the energy estimate,
Lemma

2.4
, we have

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|Du|)\;dz

\displaystyle\leq c\underbrace{{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H\left(z,\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|}{2\rho}\right)dz}_{\mathrm{I}}+c\underbrace{\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{\lambda^{2}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{2}}{(2\rho)^{2}}\;dz}_{\mathrm{II}}

\displaystyle\quad+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\;dz.

Estimate of
\mathrm{I}
:
From
Lemma

3.15
, applying Young’s inequality with
\left(\frac{1}{1-\theta},\frac{1}{\theta}\right)
yields

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H\left(z,\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|}{2\rho}\right)dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\right)^{1-\theta}\left[{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz+\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz\right)^{\theta}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}\right)+c(\varepsilon)\left[\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}+{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz\right].

Estimate of
\mathrm{II}
:
We estimate
\mathrm{II}
by considering it in two separate parts. We proceed as follows:

\displaystyle\underbrace{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p-2}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{2}}{(2\rho)^{2}}\;dz}_{\mathrm{J}_{1}}+\underbrace{b(z_{0})\lambda^{q-2}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{2}}{(2\rho)^{2}}\;dz}_{\mathrm{J}_{2}}.

First, we estimate
\mathrm{J}_{1}
by using
Lemma

2.1
with
\sigma=2,s=\theta p,\vartheta=\frac{1}{2},r=2.
Indeed,

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{1}=

\displaystyle\lambda^{p-2}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z_{0})\frac{|u-(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{2}}{(2\rho)^{2}}\;dz

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{lem : lemma 2.1}}{\leq}c\lambda^{p-2}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z_{0})^{\theta}\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{\theta p}}{(2\rho)^{\theta p}}+a(z_{0})^{\theta}|Du|^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta p}}a(z_{0})^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\left(S(u)^{(p,q)}_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{Lemma 3.14}}{\leq}c\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z_{0})^{\theta}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta p}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq c\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\theta}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta p}}.

Now applying Young’s inequality with
(p,\frac{p}{p-1}),
we obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+c(\varepsilon)\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}a(z)^{\theta}\left(|Du|+|F|\right)^{\theta p}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+c(\varepsilon)\left[\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}+{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz\right].

Similarly, we can estimate
\mathrm{J}_{2}
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathrm{J}_{2}\leq\varepsilon b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}+c(\varepsilon)\left[\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}+{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz\right].

Thus, combining the above estimates gives

\displaystyle\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}}{\lambda^{2}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}\frac{|u-(u)_{z_{0},2\rho,\lambda}|^{2}}{(2\rho)^{2}}\;dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq\varepsilon(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q})+c\left[\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)}}H(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}+{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\,dz\right].

Finally, using the fourth condition of (
3.10
)-(
3.11
) and absorbing
\varepsilon(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q})
to the left-hand side, we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

4.
Gradient higher integrability

In this section, our goal is to prove
Theorem

1.1
. Note that
Theorem

1.1
is stated in terms of
H_{1}
, as
H_{1}
exhibits
(p,q)
-growth, as established in
Lemma

2.3
. However, so far, the reverse Hölder inequalities have only been derived for
H
. Extending these inequalities to
H_{1}
is straightforward, which is the content of our next lemma. Below, we present the proof of the
p
-intrinsic case, while the
q
-intrinsic and
(p,q)
-intrinsic cases follow in the same way.

Lemma 4.1

(Reverse Hölder inequality in terms of
H_{1}
)
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Then for
Q_{c_{v}(2\rho),\lambda}\left(z_{0}\right)\subset\Omega_{T}
with (
3.1
), there exist constants
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})\geq 1
and
\theta_{0}=\theta_{0}(n,p,q)\in(0,1)
such that for any
\theta\in(\theta_{0},1),

(4.1)

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\;dz\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)]^{\theta}\;dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|F|)\;dz.

Proof.

From
Lemma

3.6
, we have

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|Du|)\;dz\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}[H(z,|Du|)]^{\theta}\;dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H(z,|F|)\;dz.

Adding
1
on both sides of the above inequality and using the fact that

0\leq H(z,|Du|)\leq H_{1}(z,|Du|),

we get the desired estimate (
4.1
).
∎

4.1.
Stopping time argument

Recall that

H_{1}(z,\kappa)=a(z)\kappa^{p}+b(z)\kappa^{q}+1

and we set

(4.2)

\lambda_{0}^{2}:={{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\;dz

and

(4.3)

\Lambda_{0}:=\sup_{z\in Q_{2r}(z_{0})}a(z)\lambda_{0}^{p}+\sup_{z\in Q_{2r}(z_{0})}b(z)\lambda_{0}^{q}+1.

Also, for a fixed
\delta>0
satisfying
\delta\leq a(z)+b(z),
we set

(4.4)

\displaystyle K:=2+\frac{120[b]_{\alpha}}{\delta}\left(\frac{1}{2|B_{1}|}\iint_{\Omega_{T}}[H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)]\,dz\right)^{\frac{\gamma}{p}}

with

\gamma:=\frac{\alpha p}{n+2}.

We define
c_{v}\geq c_{*}(\texttt{data})
by

(4.5)

c_{v}:=c_{*}(\texttt{data})K,

where
c_{*}(\texttt{data})
will be determined later.
For
\Lambda_{1}:=\Lambda+1,
we define the superlevel sets

(4.6)

\displaystyle E(\Lambda_{1})=\left\{z\in\Omega_{T}:\,H_{1}\left(z,|Du(z)|\right)>\Lambda_{1}\right\}

and

(4.7)

\displaystyle\Phi(\Lambda_{1})=\left\{z\in\Omega_{T}:\,H_{1}\left(z,|F(z)|\right)>\Lambda_{1}\right\}.

For
E(\Lambda_{1}),\;\Phi(\Lambda_{1})
and
\rho\in[r,2r]
, we write

(4.8)

\displaystyle E(\Lambda_{1},\rho):=E(\Lambda_{1})\cap Q_{\rho}(z_{0})=\left\{z\in Q_{\rho}(z_{0}):\,H_{1}(z,|Du(z)|)>\Lambda_{1})\right\}

and

\displaystyle\Phi(\Lambda_{1},\rho):=\Phi(\Lambda_{1})\cap Q_{\rho}(z_{0})=\left\{z\in Q_{\rho}(z_{0}):\,H_{1}(z,|F(z)|)>\Lambda_{1})\right\}.

Now, we discuss the stopping time argument. We want to mention that our plan differs slightly from that in
[
22
, Section 5.1]
. Here, we need to treat the
p
-intrinsic and
q
-intrinsic cases independently. To be more precise, we obtain a stopping time radius
\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}>0
from the
p
-intrinsic case and another stopping time radius
\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}>0
from the
q
-intrinsic case. Finally, we find a smaller radius
\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}}<\min\{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}\}
as a stopping time for the
(p,q)
-intrinsic case.

Let
r\leq r_{1}<r_{2}\leq 2r
, and let

(4.9)

\Lambda_{1}>\left(\frac{4c_{v}r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{q(n+2)}{2}}\Lambda_{0}.

For each
\mathfrak{z}\in E(\Lambda_{1},r_{1})
, we take
\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}>0
such that

(4.10)

\Lambda_{1}=a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{p}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q}+1.

For
\mathfrak{z}\in E(\Lambda_{1},r_{1})
and
K>1
, we have the following possibilities:

(4.11)

\displaystyle(1)\,\,\,\,Ka(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}\geq b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}},\quad(2)\,\,\,\,Ka(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}.

4.1.1.
Existence of
p
-intrinsic case (
3.1
)

We consider the first possibility of (
4.11
). We claim that

(4.12)

\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}>\left(\frac{4c_{v}r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{n+2}{2}}\lambda_{0}\;(\geq 1).

Suppose not, then we have

\displaystyle\Lambda_{1}=a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{p}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q}+1

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{4c_{v}r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{q(n+2)}{2}}(a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{0}^{p}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{0}^{q}+1)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{4c_{v}r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{q(n+2)}{2}}\Lambda_{0},

which contradicts (
4.9
). Thus, for
\tau\in[(r_{2}-r_{1})/(2c_{v}),r_{2}-r_{1})
, we have

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\;dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{p-2}\left(\frac{2r}{\tau}\right)^{n+2}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\;dz

(4.13)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left(\frac{4c_{v}r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{n+2}a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{p-2}\lambda_{0}^{2}\overset{\eqref{eq: lambda bigger than 1}}{<}a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{p}<a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{p}+2.

The facts
\mathfrak{z}\in E(\Lambda_{1},r_{1})
and (
4.10
) imply that
\mathfrak{z}\in E(a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{p}+1,r_{1})
. We define a continuous function
\Phi:(0,r_{2}-r_{1}]\to\mathbb{R}
as

\displaystyle\Phi(\tau)={{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\;dz.

By the Lebesgue differentiation theorem, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\tau\to 0}\Phi(\tau)=H_{1}(\mathfrak{z},|Du(\mathfrak{z})|)+H_{1}(\mathfrak{z},|F(\mathfrak{z})|)>a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+2.

Therefore, by intermediate value property of continuous function, there exists
\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\in(0,(r_{2}-r_{1})/(2c_{v}))
such that

(4.14)

{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\;dz=a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{p}+2

holds. Moreover, from (
4.1.2
) we conclude

(4.15)

{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\;dz<a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{p}+2

for every
\tau\in(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},r_{2}-r_{1})
. From (
4.15
) and (
4.14
), we get the third and fourth conditions of (
3.1
).
Now for this
\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}>0,
we have four possibilities:

(i)

\,\,\,a(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}),\,\,\,b(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2[b]_{\alpha}\left(10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\right)^{\alpha},

(ii)

\,\,\,a(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}),\,\,\,b(\mathfrak{z})\leq 2[b]_{\alpha}\left(10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\right)^{\alpha},

(iii)

\,\,\,a(\mathfrak{z})\leq 2\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}),\,\,\,b(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2[b]_{\alpha}\left(10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\right)^{\alpha},

(iv)

\,\,\,a(\mathfrak{z})\leq 2\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}),\,\,\,b(\mathfrak{z})\leq 2[b]_{\alpha}\left(10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\right)^{\alpha}.

We show that the case (ii) implies
p
-intrinsic case (
3.1
). For small
\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},
using the second condition of (ii), we get

\displaystyle\delta\leq a(\mathfrak{z})+b(\mathfrak{z})\leq a(\mathfrak{z})+2[b]_{\alpha}(10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{\alpha}<a(\mathfrak{z})+\frac{\delta}{2},

we conclude that
a(\mathfrak{z})\geq\frac{\delta}{2}
which implies first part of the second condition of (
3.1
) replacing the center point. We note that, the smallness of
\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}
will be guaranteed later, for instance, we can choose large
c_{v}
given by (
4.5
) to get
\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}
as small as we want. Moreover, from the first condition of (ii), for any
z\in Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})
we have

\displaystyle a(z)\leq|a(z)-a(\mathfrak{z})|+a(\mathfrak{z})\leq\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})+a(\mathfrak{z})\leq\frac{1}{2}a(\mathfrak{z})+a(\mathfrak{z})\leq 3a(\mathfrak{z}).

Similarly, we get
a(\mathfrak{z})\leq 3a(z)
for any
z\in Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z}).
This implies the second part of the second condition of (
3.1
) replacing the center and the radius by
\mathfrak{z}
and
\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}
respectively.

It is easy to see from the condition
\delta\leq a(\mathfrak{z})+b(\mathfrak{z})
that (iv) cannot occur. Also, (iii) is not possible for the following reason: in this case
a(\mathfrak{z})\leq 2\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})
and for small
\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},
it can be assumed that
\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})<\frac{\delta}{4K}
. Thus, we have
a(\mathfrak{z})<\frac{\delta}{2K}
. From this, we see that
b(\mathfrak{z})\geq\frac{2K-1}{2K}\delta
, and hence

\frac{(2K-1)\delta}{2K}\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q}\leq b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q}\leq Ka(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{p}\leq 2K\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{p}.

Since for small
\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}>0,
we have
\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})<\frac{\delta}{4K},
then we arrive at

\frac{(2K-1)\delta}{2K}<\frac{\delta}{2}\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{p-q}\leq\frac{\delta}{2},

which is a contradiction as this gives
K<1.
This completes the existence of
p
-intrinsic case.

4.1.2.
Existence of
q
-intrinsic case (
3.6
)

We consider the second possibility of (
4.11
). Since (
4.12
) holds for
\tau\in[(r_{2}-r_{1})/(2c_{v}),r_{2}-r_{1})
, we have

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\;dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q-2}\left(\frac{2r}{\tau}\right)^{n+2}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\;dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left(\frac{4c_{v}r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{n+2}b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q-2}\lambda_{0}^{2}\overset{\eqref{eq: lambda bigger than 1}}{<}b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q}<b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q}+2.

The facts
\mathfrak{z}\in E(\Lambda_{1},r_{1})
and (
4.10
) imply that
\mathfrak{z}\in E(b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q}+1,r_{1})
. By the Lebesgue differentiation theorem, there exists
\rho_{q;\,\mathfrak{z}}\in(0,(r_{2}-r_{1})/(2c_{v}))
such that

(4.16)

{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\;dz=b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q}+2

and

(4.17)

{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\;dz<b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q}+2

for every
\tau\in(\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},r_{2}-r_{1}).
From (
4.16
) and (
4.17
), we get the third and fourth conditions of (
3.6
) by replacing the center and radius with
\mathfrak{z}
and
\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}.

Now that the radius
\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}>0
is defined, we have four possibilities.

(i)

\,\,\,a(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2\omega_{a}(\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}),\,\,\,b(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2[b]_{\alpha}\left(10\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}\right)^{\alpha},

(ii)

\,\,\,a(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2\omega_{a}(\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}),\,\,\,b(\mathfrak{z})\leq 2[b]_{\alpha}\left(10\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}\right)^{\alpha},

(iii)

\,\,\,a(\mathfrak{z})\leq 2\omega_{a}(\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}),\,\,\,b(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2[b]_{\alpha}\left(10\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}\right)^{\alpha},

(iv)

\,\,\,a(\mathfrak{z})\leq 2\omega_{a}(\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}),\,\,\,b(\mathfrak{z})\leq 2[b]_{\alpha}\left(10\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}\right)^{\alpha}.

Now we show that (iii) implies
q
-intrinsic case (
3.6
). Since, for small
\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}
we may assume
\omega_{a}(\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}})\leq\frac{\delta}{4},
from the first condition of (iii), we get

\displaystyle\delta\leq a(\mathfrak{z})+b(\mathfrak{z})\leq 2\omega_{a}(\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}})+b(\mathfrak{z})\leq\frac{\delta}{2}+b(\mathfrak{z}),

which gives us the lower bound of
b(z_{0})
in (
3.6
) replacing the center with
\mathfrak{z}.
The second condition of (iii) implies the second part of the second condition of (
3.6
). Indeed, for any
z\in Q_{10\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z}),
we have

\displaystyle b(z)\leq|b(z)-b(\mathfrak{z})|+b(\mathfrak{z})

\displaystyle\leq[b]_{\alpha}(10\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}})^{\alpha}+b(\mathfrak{z})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{b(\mathfrak{z})}{2}+b(\mathfrak{z})\leq 2b(\mathfrak{z}).

We note that (iv) cannot occur because
a(\mathfrak{z})+b(\mathfrak{z})\geq\delta>0.
We show (ii) cannot occur. First, we note that using the second condition of (ii), we get
a(\mathfrak{z})\geq\frac{\delta}{2}
as before. It follows from (
4.16
) that

\displaystyle b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}={{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}[H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)]\;dz<\left(\frac{K\delta}{120[b]_{\alpha}}\right)^{\frac{n+2}{\alpha}}\frac{1}{|J_{\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}|}=\left(\frac{K\delta}{120[b]_{\alpha}}\right)^{\frac{n+2}{\alpha}}\frac{b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q-2}_{\mathfrak{z}}}{\rho^{n+2}_{q;\mathfrak{z}}}.

This implies

\displaystyle\rho^{\alpha}_{q;\mathfrak{z}}\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}<\frac{K\delta}{120[b]_{\alpha}}\lambda^{q-\frac{2\alpha}{n+2}}_{\mathfrak{z}},

and using the range of
q
, given in (
1.6
), i.e.,
q\leq p+\frac{2\alpha}{n+2}
, we get

(4.18)

\displaystyle\rho^{\alpha}_{q;\mathfrak{z}}\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}<\frac{K}{40[b]_{\alpha}}\frac{\delta}{3}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}.

Now using second possibility of (
4.11
) and second condition of (ii), we conclude

(4.19)

\displaystyle Ka(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq 2[b]_{\alpha}(10\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}})^{\alpha}\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}<\frac{K10^{\alpha}}{20}\frac{\delta}{3}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}.

On the other hand, we have

(4.20)

\displaystyle Ka(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}\geq\frac{K\delta}{2}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}.

Combining the inequalities (
4.19
) and (
4.20
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{K\delta}{2}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}<\frac{K\delta}{6}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}},

which is a contradiction.

Note that so far we have not considered the possibilities
