\displaystyle\,\,\,a(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}),\,\,\,b(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2[b]_{\alpha}\left(10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\right)^{\alpha},\quad\text{and}\quad a(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2\omega_{a}(\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}),\,\,\,b(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2[b]_{\alpha}\left(10\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}\right)^{\alpha},

appearing in
p
- and
q
-intrinsic cases and now these possibilities are useful to define
(p,q)
-intrinsic case. Define

\widetilde{\rho}_{\mathfrak{z}}=\min\{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}\}.

From the above two possibilities, we have

\displaystyle a(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2\omega_{a}(\widetilde{\rho}_{\mathfrak{z}}),\quad b(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2[b]_{\alpha}\left(10\widetilde{\rho}_{\mathfrak{z}}\right)^{\alpha}.

4.1.3.
Existence of
(p,q)
-intrinsic case (
3.10
)–(
3.11
)

In this case, we consider, any of the possibilities (1)
or (2) of (
4.11
), i.e.,

\displaystyle Ka(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}\geq b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}},\quad\text{or}\quad Ka(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}

and

(4.21)

\displaystyle a(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2\omega_{a}(\widetilde{\rho}_{\mathfrak{z}}),\quad b(\mathfrak{z})\geq 2[b]_{\alpha}\left(10\widetilde{\rho}_{\mathfrak{z}}\right)^{\alpha}.

Since
G_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})\subset Q_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})
and
G_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})\subset J_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z}),
from (
4.15
) and (
4.17
), we obtain

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)\,dz

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}}{a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)\,dz

\displaystyle<a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}\quad\text{for every}\quad\tau\in(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},r_{2}-r_{1}).

This gives

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz<a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}+2\quad\text{for every}\quad\tau\in(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},r_{2}-r_{1}).

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)\,dz

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}}{b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptJ_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)\,dz

\displaystyle<a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}\quad\text{for every}\quad\tau\in(\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},r_{2}-r_{1}).

Similarly, this gives

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz<a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}+2\quad\text{for every}\quad\tau\in(\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},r_{2}-r_{1}).

Since
\mathfrak{z}\in E(\Lambda_{1},r_{1})
and
\Lambda_{1}=a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}+1,
from the above two assertions, we get
\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}}\in(0,\widetilde{\rho}_{\mathfrak{z}}]
such that

(4.22)

{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\;dz=a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{z}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q}+2

and

(4.23)

{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptG_{\tau,\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\;dz<a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{z}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}^{q}+2

for every
\tau\in(\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}},r_{2}-r_{1}).

Also, from (
4.21
), we get
a(z)\leq 3a(\mathfrak{z})
for all
z\in Q_{\widetilde{\rho}_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})
and
b(z)\leq 3b(\mathfrak{z})
for all
z\in J_{\widetilde{\rho}_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z}).
Since, by definition,
G_{\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})\subset Q_{\widetilde{\rho}_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})\cap J_{\widetilde{\rho}_{q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z}),
we get the second assumption of (
3.10
)-(
3.11
) by replacing the center and the radius by
\mathfrak{z}
and
\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}}.

4.2.
Vitali covering lemma

In Section
3
and Subsection
4.1
, we considered three distinct intrinsic cases to obtain reverse Hölder inequalities.
In this subsection, we establish a Vitali type covering lemma for the following parabolic intrinsic cylinders: For each
\mathfrak{z}\in E(\Lambda,r_{1})
, we denote the intrinsic cylinder of
\mathfrak{z}
by

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})\quad\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\text{in $p$-intrinsic case},\\
J_{2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})\quad\,\,\,\,\,\,\text{in $q$-intrinsic case},\\
G_{2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})\quad\,\text{in $(p,q)$-intrinsic case}.\end{array}\right.

Let us define

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}=\left\{\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})\,:\,\mathfrak{z}\in E(\Lambda_{1},r_{1})\right\}\quad\text{and}\quad\ell(\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z}))=\left\{\begin{array}[]{l }2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\quad\,\,\,\,\,\,\text{in $p$-intrinsic case},\\
2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}\quad\,\,\,\text{in $q$-intrinsic case},\\
2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}}\quad\text{in $(p,q)$-intrinsic case}.\end{array}\right.

Also, for
\mathfrak{z},\mathfrak{w}\in E(\Lambda_{1},r_{1}),
there exist
\lambda_{i}\geq 1
and
\rho_{i}>0
such that

\displaystyle\Lambda_{1}=a(i)\lambda^{p}_{i}+b(i)\lambda^{q}_{i}+1,\quad\text{where}\ \,i\in\left\{\mathfrak{z},\mathfrak{w}\right\},

and

(4.24)

{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{\rho_{i},\lambda_{i}}(i)}}\left[H(z,|Du|)+H(z,|F|)\right]\;dz=a(i)\lambda_{i}^{p}.

We note from the previous subsection that
l(\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z}))\in(0,R)
by setting
R:=\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{c_{v}}
, where
c_{v}
is given by (
4.5
). Now we consider the following subcollection of
\mathcal{F},

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{j}:=\left\{\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})\in\mathcal{F}:\frac{R}{2^{j}}<\ell({\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})})\leq\frac{R}{2^{j-1}}\right\},\,\,\,\,j\in\mathbb{N}.

We construct disjoint subcollections
\mathcal{I}_{j}\subset\mathcal{F}_{j}
for
j\in\mathbb{N}
as follows. Let
\mathcal{I}_{1}
be the maximal disjoint subcollection in
\mathcal{F}_{1}.
We note from the following

\displaystyle\lim_{\Lambda_{1}\to\infty}\Lambda_{1}|E(\Lambda_{1})|\leq\lim_{\Lambda_{1}\to\infty}\iint_{E(\Lambda_{1})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz=0

that
|E(\Lambda_{1})|<\infty.

Moreover, in the same way as in (
4.1.2
) and (
4.14
), we have

(4.25)

\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq\left(\frac{2r}{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}\right)^{\frac{n+2}{2}}\lambda_{0}.

Using
|E(\Lambda_{1},r_{1})|\leq|E(\Lambda_{1})|<\infty
and (
4.25
), we conclude that the number of cylinders in
\mathcal{I}_{1}
is finite. Suppose we have already chosen
\mathcal{I}_{j}\subset\mathcal{F}_{j}
for
j=1,..,k-1.
Then we construct
\mathcal{I}_{k}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{k}=\left\{\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})\in\mathcal{F}_{k}\,:\,\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})=\emptyset\,\,\,\text{for every}\,\,\,\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})\in\bigcup_{j=1}^{k-1}\mathcal{I}_{j}\right\}.

Therefore,

\mathcal{I}=\bigcup_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathcal{I}_{j}

would be the maximal collection of pairwise disjoint cylinders in
\mathcal{F}.
Now to prove Vitali’s covering lemma, we need to show:

(i)

For any
\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})\in\mathcal{F},
there exists
\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})\in\mathcal{I}
such that
\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})\neq\emptyset.

(ii)

There exists a universal constant
c_{v}>1
such that
\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})\subset c_{v}\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})
where

c_{v}\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{l c}Q_{c_{v}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}}),\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(\mathfrak{w})\,\,\,\,\,\,\quad\text{in $p$-intrinsic case},\\
J_{c_{v}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}}),\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(\mathfrak{w})\,\,\,\,\,\quad\text{in $q$-intrinsic case},\\
G_{c_{v}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}}),\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(\mathfrak{w})\quad\text{in $(p,q)$-intrinsic case}.\end{array}\right.

To show the first assertion, fix some
Q(\mathfrak{z})\in\mathcal{F}.
Then
Q(\mathfrak{z})\in\mathcal{F}_{j}
for some
j\in\mathbb{N}.
Using the maximality of
\mathcal{I}_{j},
we find that there exists
\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})\in\cup_{i=1}^{j}\mathcal{I}_{i}
such that
\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})\neq\emptyset.

To show the second assertion, we first observe that for any
\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})\in\mathcal{I}_{i},
with
i\leq j,
we have

(4.26)

\displaystyle l(\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z}))\leq 2l(\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})).

This is due to the fact that

\frac{R}{2^{j}}\leq\frac{R}{2^{i}}<\ell(\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w}))\leq\frac{R}{2^{i-1}}
for
i\leq j
, and
\frac{R}{2^{j}}<\ell(\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z}))\leq\frac{R}{2^{j-1}}
.

In the rest of the subsection, we show the existence of the Vitali constant
c_{v}>1
such that
\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})\subset c_{v}\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})
by considering the following nine distinct cases depicted in the table below.

[formula]
[formula]

Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(\mathfrak{w})

J_{2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(\mathfrak{w})

G_{2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(\mathfrak{w})

Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})

(1-1)

(1-2)

(2-1)

(3-1)

Table 1.
The combinations of
\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})
and
\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})
.

Claim:

\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}
and
\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}
are comparable.

Since
G_{2\rho_{p,q;(\cdot)},\lambda_{(\cdot)}}(\cdot)\subset Q_{2\rho_{(\cdot)},\lambda_{(\cdot)}}(\cdot)
and
G_{2\rho_{p,q;(\cdot)},\lambda_{(\cdot)}}(\cdot)\subset J_{2\rho_{q;(\cdot)},\lambda_{(\cdot)}}(\cdot),
it is enough to consider cases (1-1), (1-2), (2-1), (2-2). Let
\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}.
We will show that
\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}\leq(2K)^{\frac{1}{p}}\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}.

Case (1-1):
First, we note from the first assertion that
Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{z})\cap Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(\mathfrak{w})\neq\emptyset.
Using the Hölder continuity of
b(\cdot)
and (
4.26
), we get

\displaystyle|b(\mathfrak{z})-b(\mathfrak{w})|\leq[b]_{\alpha}\left(2\rho^{\alpha}_{\mathfrak{z}}+2\rho^{\alpha}_{\mathfrak{w}}\right)\leq 6[b]_{\alpha}\rho^{\alpha}_{\mathfrak{w}},

and from the uniform continuity of
a(\cdot),
we have

\displaystyle|a(\mathfrak{z})-a(\mathfrak{w})|\leq\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})+\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{w}}).

On the contrary, assume
\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}>(2K)^{\frac{1}{p}}\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}.
Moreover, using (
4.24
), we can derive

(4.27)

\displaystyle\rho^{\alpha}_{\mathfrak{w}}\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}\leq\frac{K}{40[b]_{\alpha}}\frac{\delta}{3}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}

similar to the estimate (
4.18
).
Now we use the counter assumption, (
4.27
),
a(\mathfrak{w})\geq\frac{\delta}{2},
and
\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}
to obtain

\displaystyle\Lambda_{1}=a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}+1

\displaystyle\leq|a(\mathfrak{z})-a(\mathfrak{w})|\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+|b(\mathfrak{z})-b(\mathfrak{w})|\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}+1

\displaystyle\leq\left(\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})+\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})\right)\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+6[b]_{\alpha}\rho^{\alpha}_{\mathfrak{w}}\frac{\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}{(2K)^{q/p}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}+1

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{estimate on rho alpha2.0}}{\leq}\frac{K\delta}{10}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+\frac{1}{(2K)^{q/p-1}}\frac{\delta}{20}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}+1

\displaystyle<\frac{K}{5}a(\mathfrak{w})\frac{\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}{2K}+a(\mathfrak{w})\frac{\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}{2K}+\frac{1}{(2K)^{q/p-1}10}a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}+1

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{10}+\frac{1}{2K}+\frac{1}{(2K)^{q/p-1}10}\right)a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}+1

\displaystyle<\frac{7}{10}a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}+1<a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}+1=\Lambda_{1},

which is a contradiction.

Case (2-1):
Since, by (
4.26
) we have
\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}}\leq 2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}},
we can conclude this case by following the same argument as above.

Case (2-2):
Following the previous argument together with (
4.26
) and
b(\mathfrak{w})\geq\frac{\delta}{2},
, we obtain

\displaystyle\Lambda_{1}=a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}+1

\displaystyle\leq|a(\mathfrak{z})-a(\mathfrak{w})|\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+|b(\mathfrak{z})-b(\mathfrak{w})|\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}+1

\displaystyle<\left(\omega_{a}(\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}})+\omega_{a}(\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}})\right)\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+6[b]_{\alpha}\rho^{\alpha}_{q;\mathfrak{w}}\frac{\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}{(2K)^{q/p}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\frac{\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}{(2K)^{\frac{q}{p}}}+1

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{estimate on rho alpha}}{\leq}\frac{K\delta}{10}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+\frac{1}{(2K)^{q/p-1}}\frac{\delta}{20}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\frac{\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}{(2K)^{\frac{q}{p}}}+1

\displaystyle<\frac{K}{5}b(\mathfrak{w})\frac{\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}{2K}+a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+\frac{1}{(2K)^{q/p-1}}\frac{1}{10}b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\frac{\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}{(2K)^{\frac{q}{p}}}+1

\displaystyle=a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+\left(\frac{1}{10}+\frac{1}{(2K)^{\frac{q}{p}}}+\frac{1}{(2K)^{q/p-1}10}\right)b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}+1

\displaystyle<a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+\frac{7}{10}b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}+1<a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}+1=\Lambda_{1},

which yields a contradiction.

Case (1-2):
Since, by (
4.26
) we have
\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq 2\rho_{q,\mathfrak{w}},
we can conclude this case by following the same argument as above.

Note that (
4.18
) is proved in the
p
-intrinsic case, but the same assertion can be proved for the
q
-intrinsic case as well.

Assuming
\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}\leq\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}},
by an argument similar to above, we can show
\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq(2K)^{\frac{1}{p}}\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}
in all the above cases. This completes the proof of the comparability of the scaling factors.

4.2.1.
Space inclusion

The inclusion of the space part follows from properties
\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})\neq\emptyset
and (
4.26
). We only show case (1-1) in
Table

1
and other cases follow similarly. Let
\mathfrak{z}=(x_{\mathfrak{z}},t_{\mathfrak{z}})
and
\mathfrak{w}=(x_{\mathfrak{w}},t_{\mathfrak{w}})
and we have
B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}(x_{\mathfrak{z}})\cap B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}}}(x_{\mathfrak{w}})\neq\emptyset.
For
x\in B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}(x_{\mathfrak{z}}),
we have

\displaystyle|x-x_{\mathfrak{w}}|\leq|x-x_{\mathfrak{z}}|+|x_{\mathfrak{z}}-x_{\mathfrak{w}}|\leq 2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}+2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}+2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}}=4\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}+2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}}\overset{\eqref{radius compare}}{\leq}10\rho_{\mathfrak{w}}\leq c_{v}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}}).

Choosing
c_{*}(\texttt{data})\geq\frac{5}{K},
we have the conclusion that
x\in B_{2c_{v}\rho_{\mathfrak{w}}}(x_{\mathfrak{w}}).

4.2.2.
Time inclusion

For the time part, we need to consider several cases.

Case (1-1):
In this case, we have

\displaystyle I_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})\cap I_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(t_{\mathfrak{w}})\neq\emptyset.

Let
t\in I_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}}).
Note that for
c_{v}=c_{*}(\texttt{data})K\geq c_{*}(\texttt{data}),
we have
\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})+\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})\leq\frac{\delta}{2}.
Using this and
a(\mathfrak{z})\geq\frac{\delta}{2},
we have

\displaystyle a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}

\displaystyle\leq|a(\mathfrak{w})-a(\mathfrak{z})|\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}

(4.28)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})+\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})\right)\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}\leq\frac{\delta}{2}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}\leq 2a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}\leq 4Ka(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}.

Then, using (
4.28
) we get

\displaystyle|t-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|\leq|t-t_{\mathfrak{z}}|+|t_{\mathfrak{z}}-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}a(\mathfrak{z})}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}a(\mathfrak{w})}

(4.29)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{2}(4K)}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}a(\mathfrak{w})}.

Using
\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq(2K)^{\frac{1}{p}}\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}
and
\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq 2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}}
in (
4.29
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{2\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{2}(4K)}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}a(\mathfrak{w})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2(2K)^{2/p}\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}4(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}4K}{\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}a(\mathfrak{w})}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}a(\mathfrak{w})}

\displaystyle\leq\left(64K^{2}+1\right)\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}a(\mathfrak{w})}\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2c_{v}\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}a(\mathfrak{w})}

if

(4.30)

\displaystyle c_{*}(\texttt{data})\geq\frac{\sqrt{64K^{2}+1}}{K}.

Case (1-2):
In this case, we have

\displaystyle I_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})\cap I^{q}_{2\rho_{q,\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(t_{\mathfrak{w}})\neq\emptyset.

Here we first show that

(4.31)

\displaystyle b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}\lesssim a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}.

Indeed, we have

\displaystyle b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}\leq|b(\mathfrak{w})-b(\mathfrak{z})|\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}

\displaystyle\leq 6[b]_{\alpha}\rho^{\alpha}_{q;\mathfrak{w}}\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{estimate on rho alpha}}{\leq}\frac{K\delta}{20}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+(2K)^{\frac{q}{p}}b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq: p-phase condition}}{\leq}\frac{2K^{2}}{10}a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+(2K)^{\frac{q}{p}}b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq: p-phase condition}}{\leq}\frac{2K^{2}}{10}a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+(2K)^{\frac{q}{p}}Ka(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{K^{2}}{5}+(2K)^{\frac{q}{p}+1}\right)a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq(K^{3}+(2K)^{3})a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}=9K^{3}a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}.

This proves the claim (
4.31
).
Let
t\in I_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}}).
Then, using (
4.31
), we have

\displaystyle|t-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|\leq|t-t_{\mathfrak{z}}|+|t_{\mathfrak{z}}-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\cdot 9K^{3}\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\cdot 9K^{3}(2K)^{2/p}\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(8\cdot 9K^{3}(2K)+1\right)\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2c_{v}\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

if

(4.32)

\displaystyle c_{*}(\texttt{data})\geq\frac{\sqrt{144K^{4}+1}}{K}.

Case (1-3):
In this case, we have

\displaystyle I_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})\cap I^{(p,q)}_{2\rho_{q,\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(t_{\mathfrak{w}})\neq\emptyset.

Let
z_{0}\in\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w}).
Then from (
3.1
)
and (
3.10
)–(
3.11
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{a(z_{0})}{3}\leq a(\mathfrak{z})\leq 3a(z_{0}),\frac{a(z_{0})}{3}\leq a(\mathfrak{w})\leq 3a(z_{0}),\,\,\text{and}\,\,\,\frac{b(z_{0})}{3}\leq b(\mathfrak{w})\leq 3b(z_{0}).

Thus, using the above estimate and (
4.31
), we deduce

\displaystyle a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}\leq 9(2K)a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+9K^{3}a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq(3K)^{3}a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}.

Let
t\in I_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}}).
Now we have

\displaystyle|t-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|\leq|t-t_{\mathfrak{z}}|+|t_{\mathfrak{z}}-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(3K)^{3}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8(2K)^{2/p}\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(3K)^{3}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(16K(3K)^{3}+1\right)\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2c_{v}\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

if

(4.33)

\displaystyle c_{*}(\texttt{data})\geq\frac{\sqrt{432K^{4}+1}}{K}.

Case (2-1):
In this case, we have

\displaystyle I^{q}_{2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})\cap I_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(t_{\mathfrak{w}})\neq\emptyset.

We first claim that

(4.34)

\displaystyle a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}\lesssim b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}.

Indeed, for
c_{v}=c_{*}(\texttt{data})K\geq c_{*}(\texttt{data})
, we see that

\displaystyle a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}\leq|a(\mathfrak{w})-a(\mathfrak{z})|\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})+\omega_{a}(\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})\right)\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\delta}{2}\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+(2K)a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}

\displaystyle\leq(2K)b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+(2K)\frac{b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}}{K}\leq 4Kb(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}.

Let
t\in I^{q}_{2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}}).
Then using (
4.34
), we get

\displaystyle|t-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|\leq|t-t_{\mathfrak{z}}|+|t_{\mathfrak{z}}-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}+\frac{2\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}+\frac{8K\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}+\frac{32K(2K)^{2/p}\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(64K^{2}+1\right)\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2c_{v}\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

if

(4.35)

\displaystyle c_{*}(\texttt{data})\geq\frac{\sqrt{64K^{2}+1}}{K}.

Case (2-2):
In this case, we have

\displaystyle I^{q}_{2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})\cap I^{q}_{2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(t_{\mathfrak{w}})\neq\emptyset.

We note that, for
z_{0}\in\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\mathcal{Q}(\mathfrak{w})
,

\displaystyle\frac{b(z_{0})}{3}\leq b(\mathfrak{z})\leq 3b(z_{0})\quad\text{and}\quad\frac{b(z_{0})}{3}\leq b(\mathfrak{w})\leq 3b(z_{0}).

This implies

(4.36)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}}\leq\frac{9(2K)^{q/p}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}.

Let
t\in I^{q}_{2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}}).
Then, substituting (
4.36
) gives

\displaystyle|t-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|\leq|t-t_{\mathfrak{z}}|+|t_{\mathfrak{z}}-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}+\frac{2\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}+\frac{32(2K)^{\frac{q}{p}+1}\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{q;w})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(256K^{3}+1\right)\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2c_{v}\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

if

(4.37)

\displaystyle c_{*}(\texttt{data})\geq\frac{\sqrt{256K^{3}+1}}{K}.

Case (2-3):
In this case, we have

\displaystyle I^{q}_{2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})\cap I^{(p,q)}_{2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(t_{\mathfrak{w}})\neq\emptyset.

We first note that, using (
4.34
) and (
4.36
), we get

\displaystyle a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}\leq 4Kb(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}+9(2K)^{\frac{q}{p}}b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}\leq 40K^{2}b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}.

Let
t\in I^{q}_{2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}}).
Then using this above estimate, we get

\displaystyle|t-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|\leq|t-t_{\mathfrak{z}}|+|t_{\mathfrak{z}}-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{w}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{160K^{2}(2K)^{\frac{2}{p}}\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{w}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{w}}

\displaystyle\leq(320K^{3}+1)\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{w}}\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2c_{v}\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{w}}

if

(4.38)

\displaystyle c_{*}(\texttt{data})\geq\frac{\sqrt{320K^{3}+1}}{K}.

Case (3-1):
In this case, we have

\displaystyle I^{(p,q)}_{2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})\cap I_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(t_{\mathfrak{w}})\neq\emptyset.

For
t\in I^{(p,q)}_{2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})
, we get

\displaystyle|t-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|\leq|t-t_{\mathfrak{z}}|+|t_{\mathfrak{z}}-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}.

This turns out to be the Case (1-1) with a different constant and following the calculations, we conclude
I^{(p,q)}_{2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})\subset I_{2c_{v}\rho_{\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(t_{\mathfrak{w}})
if

(4.39)

\displaystyle c_{*}(\texttt{data})\geq\frac{\sqrt{256K^{2}+1}}{K}.

Case (3-2):
In this case, we have

\displaystyle I^{(p,q)}_{2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})\cap I^{q}_{2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(t_{\mathfrak{w}})\neq\emptyset.

For
t\in I^{(p,q)}_{2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})
, we get

\displaystyle|t-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|\leq|t-t_{\mathfrak{z}}|+|t_{\mathfrak{z}}-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{q;\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}}.

This turns out to be the Case (2-1) with a different constant and following the calculations, we conclude
I^{(p,q)}_{2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})\subset I^{q}_{2c_{v}\rho_{q;\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(t_{\mathfrak{w}})
if

(4.40)

\displaystyle c_{*}(\texttt{data})\geq\frac{\sqrt{256K^{2}+1}}{K}.

Case (3-3):
In this case, we have

\displaystyle I^{(p,q)}_{2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}})\cap I^{(p,q)}_{2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{w}}}(t_{\mathfrak{w}})\neq\emptyset.

Using the comparability of
a(\cdot)
and
b(\cdot),
we observe that

\displaystyle a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}\leq 9(2K)a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+9(2K)^{\frac{q}{p}}b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}

\displaystyle\leq 18K^{2}a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+36K^{2}b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}

\displaystyle\leq 36K^{2}\left(a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}\right).

Let
t\in I^{(p,q)}_{2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}},\lambda_{\mathfrak{z}}}(t_{\mathfrak{z}}).
Using the above estimate, we get

\displaystyle|t-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|\leq|t-t_{\mathfrak{z}}|+|t_{\mathfrak{z}}-t_{\mathfrak{w}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{z}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{z}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{z}}+b(\mathfrak{z})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{z}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8(2K)^{2/p}36K^{2}\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(576K^{3}+1\right)\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}_{\mathfrak{w}}(2c_{v}\rho_{p,q;\mathfrak{w}})^{2}}{a(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{p}_{\mathfrak{w}}+b(\mathfrak{w})\lambda^{q}_{\mathfrak{w}}}

if

(4.41)

\displaystyle c_{*}(\texttt{data})\geq\frac{\sqrt{576K^{3}+1}}{K}.

Finally, comparing all the lower bounds of
c_{*}(\texttt{data}),
from time inclusion, i.e., (
4.30
), (
4.32
), (
4.33
), (
4.35
), (
4.37
), (
4.38
), (
4.39
), (
4.40
), (
4.41
) and from space inclusion, i.e.,
4/K,
we conclude that it is sufficient to take
c_{*}(\texttt{data})\geq\sqrt{\frac{4\times 576K^{4}}{K^{2}}}=48K.
The above discussion leads to the following lemma.

Lemma 4.2

(Vitali covering lemma)
.

Let
E(\Lambda_{1},r_{1})
and
c_{v}
be given by (
4.8
) and (
4.5
) respectively. Then there exists a collection
\{\mathcal{Q}({z_{i}})\}_{i\in\mathbb{N}}
of cylinders that satisfies the following.

(i)

\cup_{i\in\mathbb{N}}\,\,c_{v}\mathcal{Q}({z_{i}})=E(\Lambda_{1},r_{1})
.

(ii)

\mathcal{Q}({z_{i}})\cap\mathcal{Q}({z_{j}})=\emptyset
for every
i,j\in\mathbb{N}
with
i\neq j
.

4.3.
Proof of
Theorem

1.1

In this subsection, we complete the proof of
Theorem

1.1
. First, we derive the following lemma as a consequence of reverse Hölder inequalities.

Lemma 4.3
.

Let
u
be a weak solution to (
1.1
). Then there exist constants
c=c(\textnormal{{data}})\geq 1
and
\theta_{0}\in(0,1)
such that for any
\theta\in(\theta_{0},1),
we have

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{2c_{v}\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du(z)|)\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\Lambda_{1}^{1-\theta}\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap E\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du(z)|)^{\theta}\,dz

(4.42)

\displaystyle\quad+c\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap\Phi\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F(z)|)\,dz,

for the
p
-intrinsic case,

\displaystyle\iint_{J_{2c_{v}\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du(z)|)\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\Lambda_{1}^{1-\theta}\iint_{J_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap E\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du(z)|)^{\theta}\,dz

(4.43)

\displaystyle\quad+c\iint_{J_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap\Phi\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F(z)|)\,dz,

for the
q
-intrinsic case and

\displaystyle\iint_{G_{2c_{v}\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du(z)|)\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\Lambda_{1}^{1-\theta}\iint_{G_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap E\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du(z)|)^{\theta}\,dz

(4.44)

\displaystyle\quad+c\iint_{G_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap\Phi\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F(z)|)\,dz,

for the
(p,q)
-intrinsic case,
whenever
Q_{2c_{v}\rho,\lambda}(z_{0}),J_{2c_{v}\rho,\lambda}(z_{0}),G_{2c_{v}\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}.

Proof.

Using the third condition of (
3.1
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1-\theta}{\theta}}\leq\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz\right)^{1-\theta}<\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1\right)^{1-\theta}.

Hence, we have

(4.45)

\displaystyle\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\leq\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1\right)^{1-\theta}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz.

Now we write

\displaystyle Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})=\left(Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap E\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)\right)\cup\left(Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\setminus E\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)\right).

From the definition of
E(\Lambda_{1})
given in (
4.6
), the right-hand side of (
4.45
) can be estimated as

\displaystyle\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|Du(z)|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1\right)^{1-\theta}\frac{\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1\right)^{\theta}}{2c}\frac{\left|Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\setminus E\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)\right|}{\left|Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\right|}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1\right)^{1-\theta}}{\left|Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\right|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap E\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du(z)|)^{\theta}\,dz

(4.46)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{2c}+\frac{\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1\right)^{1-\theta}}{\left|Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\right|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap E\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du(z)|)^{\theta}\,dz.

Similarly, using the definition of
\Phi(\Lambda_{1})
given by (
4.7
), we also have

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz

(4.47)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{2c}+\frac{1}{\left|Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\right|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap\Phi\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz.

Combining the reverse Hölder inequality (see
Lemma

4.1
), (
4.46
) and (
4.47
), we see that

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{2}+\frac{c\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1\right)^{1-\theta}}{\left|Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\right|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap E\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz

(4.48)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{c}{\left|Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\right|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap\Phi\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz.

Now using the third and fourth conditions (
3.1
) in (
4.48
), we get

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2c_{v}\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}}[H_{1}(z,|Du|)+H_{1}(z,|F|)]\,dz

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2c\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1\right)^{1-\theta}}{\left|Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\right|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap E\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2c}{\left|Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\right|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap\Phi\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz.

Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{2c_{v}\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz

\displaystyle\leq 2c\left(a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1\right)^{1-\theta}c^{n+2}_{v}\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap E\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz

(4.49)

\displaystyle\quad+2cc^{n+2}_{v}\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap\Phi\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz.

Using
Ka(z_{0})\lambda^{p}\geq b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}
of (
3.1
) and
K\geq 1,
we get
a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1\geq\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+b(z_{0})\lambda^{q}+1}{2K}=\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{2K}.
Hence we have
\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}\geq\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta_{0}}}\geq\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{2K(2c)^{1/\theta_{0}}}.
This gives that
E\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)\subset E\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{2K(2c)^{1/\theta_{0}}}}\right).
On the other hand, we also have
E\left({\frac{a(z_{0})\lambda^{p}+1}{(2c)^{1/\theta}}}\right)\subset E\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{2K(2cc^{n+2}_{v})^{1/\theta_{0}}}\right).
Also, similar inclusions hold for
\Phi(\cdot).
Now by setting
\max\left\{2c,2cc^{n+2}_{v}\right\}=:c_{1}=2cc^{n+2}_{v}
, we obtain from (
4.49
) that

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{2c_{v}\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c_{1}\Lambda_{1}^{1-\theta}\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap E\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz

\displaystyle\quad+c_{1}\iint_{Q_{2\rho,\lambda}(z_{0})\cap\Phi\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz,

which completes the proof of (
4.42
). The estimates in (
4.43
) and (
4.44
) can be obtained similarly.
∎

Now we are ready to prove
Theorem

1.1
. We present a detailed proof for the sake of completeness.

Proof of
Theorem

1.1
.

Following
Lemma

4.2
, we have a countable pairwise disjoint collection
\mathcal{I}:=\left\{\mathcal{Q}({z_{j}})\right\}_{j=1}^{\infty}
for
z_{j}\in E\left({\Lambda_{1},r_{1}}\right).
From
Lemma

4.3
, there exist constants
c\geq 1
and
\theta_{0}\in(0,1)
such that

\displaystyle\iint_{c_{v}\mathcal{Q}(z_{j})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz\leq c\Lambda_{1}^{1-\theta}\iint_{\mathcal{Q}(z_{j})\cap E\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz+c\iint_{\mathcal{Q}(z_{j})\cap\Phi\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz

holds for every
j\in\mathbb{N}
and
\theta\in(\theta_{0},1).
Since the cylinders in
\mathcal{I}
are pairwise disjoint, we get

\displaystyle\iint_{E\left({\Lambda_{1},r_{1}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\iint_{c_{v}\mathcal{Q}(z_{j})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq c\Lambda_{1}^{1-\theta}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\iint_{\mathcal{Q}(z_{j})\cap E\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz+c\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\iint_{\mathcal{Q}(z_{j})\cap\Phi\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz

(4.50)

\displaystyle\quad\leq c\Lambda_{1}^{1-\theta}\iint_{E\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c},r_{2}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz+c\iint_{\Phi\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c},r_{2}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\iint_{E\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c},r_{1}}\right)\setminus E\left({\Lambda_{1},r_{1}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz

\displaystyle=\iint_{E\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c},r_{1}}\right)\setminus E\left({\Lambda_{1},r_{1}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{1-\theta}\,dz

(4.51)

\displaystyle\leq\Lambda_{1}^{1-\theta}\iint_{E\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c}},r_{2}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz.

Now combining (
4.50
) and (
4.51
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\iint_{E\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c},r_{1}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz

\displaystyle=\iint_{E\left({\Lambda_{1},r_{1}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz+\iint_{E\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c},r_{1}}\right)\setminus E\left({\Lambda_{1},r_{1}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz

(4.52)

\displaystyle\leq c\Lambda_{1}^{1-\theta}\iint_{E\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c},r_{2}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz+c\iint_{\Phi\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c},r_{2}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz.

For any
k\in\mathbb{N},
we define the truncation operator as

\displaystyle H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}=\min\left\{H_{1}(z,|Du|),k\right\}

and the superlevel set as

\displaystyle E^{k}\left({\Lambda_{1},\uprho}\right):=\left\{z\in Q_{\uprho}(z_{0}):H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}>\Lambda_{1}\right\}.

From the above definitions, we have

(4.53)

\displaystyle E^{k}\left({\Lambda_{1},\uprho}\right)=\begin{cases}E\left({\Lambda_{1},\uprho}\right)\,\,\,\,&\text{if}\,\,\,\Lambda_{1}\leq k,\\
\emptyset\,\,\,\,&\text{if}\,\,\,\Lambda_{1}>k.\end{cases}

Therefore, we deduce from (
4.52
) and (
4.53
) that

\displaystyle\iint_{E^{k}\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c},r_{1}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}^{1-\theta}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz

\displaystyle\leq\iint_{E\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c},r_{1}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\Lambda_{1}^{1-\theta}\iint_{E\left({\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c},r_{2}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz+c\iint_{\Phi\left(\frac{\Lambda_{1}}{c},r_{2}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz.

Let us denote

\displaystyle\Lambda_{2}=\frac{1}{c}\left(\frac{4c_{v}r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{\frac{q(n+2)}{2}}\Lambda_{0}.

Then for any
\Lambda_{1}>\Lambda_{2},
we have

\displaystyle\iint_{E^{k}\left({\Lambda_{1},r_{1}}\right)}

\displaystyle H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}^{1-\theta}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz

(4.54)

\displaystyle\leq c\Lambda_{1}^{1-\theta}\iint_{E^{k}\left({\Lambda_{1},r_{2}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz+c\iint_{\Phi\left(\Lambda_{1},r_{2}\right)}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz.

Let
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
, which is to be determined later. Multiplying the above inequality (
4.54
) with
\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon-1}
and integrating over
(\Lambda_{2},\infty)
, we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda_{2}}^{\infty}\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon-1}\iint_{E^{k}\left({\Lambda_{1},r_{1}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}^{1-\theta}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz\,d\Lambda_{1}

(4.55)

\displaystyle\quad\leq c\int_{\Lambda_{2}}^{\infty}\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon-\theta}\iint_{E^{k}\left({\Lambda_{1},r_{2}}\right)}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz\,d\Lambda_{1}+c\int_{\Lambda_{2}}^{\infty}\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon-1}\iint_{\Phi(\Lambda_{1},r_{2})}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz\,d\Lambda_{1}.

Now following
[
22
]
and using Fubini’s theorem, we obtain from (
4.55
) that

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{r_{1}}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}^{1-\theta+\varepsilon}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz

\displaystyle\leq\Lambda^{\varepsilon}_{2}\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}^{1-\theta}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz+\frac{c\varepsilon}{1+\varepsilon-\theta}\iint_{Q_{r_{2}}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}^{1-\theta+\varepsilon}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz

\displaystyle\qquad+c\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|F|)^{1+\varepsilon}\,dz.

We now choose a small
\varepsilon<\varepsilon_{0}<1
such that
\frac{c\varepsilon}{1+\varepsilon-\theta}\leq\frac{1}{2}.
To use
Lemma

2.2
, we define

\displaystyle h(r)=\iint_{Q_{r}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}^{1-\theta+\varepsilon}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz.

Considering the choice of
\Lambda_{2},
we have

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\varepsilon}_{2}\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}^{1-\theta}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(r_{2}-r_{1})^{\frac{\varepsilon q(n+2)}{2}}}\left[\frac{(4c_{v}r)^{\frac{\varepsilon q(n+2)}{2}}}{c^{\varepsilon}}\Lambda^{\varepsilon}_{0}\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}^{1-\theta}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}\,dz\right]

\displaystyle=:\frac{A}{(r_{2}-r_{1})^{\frac{\varepsilon s(n+2)}{2}}}.

Now applying
Lemma

2.2
with
\varrho_{1}=r_{1},\varrho_{2}=r_{2},R=2r,\vartheta=\frac{c\varepsilon}{1+\varepsilon-\theta},B=0
and
\gamma=\frac{\varepsilon q(n+2)}{2},
we get

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{r}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}^{1-\theta+\varepsilon}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{r^{\frac{\varepsilon q(n+2)}{2}}}\frac{(4c_{v}r)^{\frac{\varepsilon q(n+2)}{2}}}{c^{\varepsilon}}\Lambda^{\varepsilon}_{0}\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)_{k}^{1-\theta}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{\theta}dz+c\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|F|)^{1+\varepsilon}\,dz.

Letting
k\to\infty,
we finally obtain

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{r}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{1+\varepsilon}\,dz\leq c\Lambda^{\varepsilon}_{0}\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz+c\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}H_{1}(z,|F|)^{1+\varepsilon}\,dz.

Recalling (
4.2
)–(
4.3
) and using
H_{1}(z,|Du|)\geq 1
with
q\geq 2,
we derive

\displaystyle{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{r}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)^{1+\varepsilon}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left(1+||a||_{L^{\infty}(\Omega_{T})}+||b||_{L^{\infty}(\Omega_{T})}\right)\lambda^{\varepsilon q}_{0}{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|F|)^{1+\varepsilon}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz\right)^{1+\frac{\varepsilon q}{2}}+c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|F|)\,dz\right)^{1+\frac{\varepsilon q}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|F|)^{1+\varepsilon}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz\right)^{1+\frac{\varepsilon q}{2}}+c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|F|)^{1+\varepsilon}\,dz\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\varepsilon}+\frac{\varepsilon}{1+\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{q}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+c{{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|F|)^{1+\varepsilon}\,dz

\displaystyle\leq c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|Du|)\,dz\right)^{1+\frac{\varepsilon q}{2}}+c\left({{{\int\hskip-7.96674pt\int}\hskip-15.93347pt-\hskip-4.26773pt-}_{\hskip-3.69899ptQ_{2r}(z_{0})}}H_{1}(z,|F|)^{1+\varepsilon}\,dz\right)^{\frac{q}{2}},

which completes the proof.
∎
